Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the bayesian structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 518. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 648 and converges at rate O(1/n^4).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the bayesian decomposition. By minimality, the element a^518 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 648-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
